MU | positive | Micron is rated positively based on record fiscal 2026 revenue of $133.19 billion, $100 billion in contracted minimum revenue locked in through 16 multiyear take-or-pay agreements with customers, $22 billion in customer cash deposits securing supply commitments, strong AI data center memory demand, quarterly dividend payments, and predictable revenue visibility extending years into the future.

SpaceX aims to generate $1 trillion in revenue by 2030 with strong growth in its Super Intelligence AI segment, but the analyst recommends staying away from the stock. Despite impressive financial results and massive opportunities, the stock's valuation at 56.5x sales leaves no room for error. Risks include uncertain revenue targets, potential AI industry slowdown, regulatory headwinds, and Starship development challenges.

Additional risks include potential AI industry slowdown, regulatory headwinds from lawmakers opposing data center expansion, and Starship development uncertainties. The current price offers no margin of safety.
